  refDoc?: string // reference document (vendor invoice, reversed document)
  poNumber?: string // purchasing document
  currency?: string // object currency (USD only)
}

/** Columns read from an extract: every SapRow field plus fiscal year (combined with a bare period number). */
export type SapColumn = keyof SapRow | 'fiscalYear'

/** Minimal RFC-4180 CSV reader (quotes, embedded commas/newlines, CRLF). Also accepts tab- or semicolon-delimited text. */
export function readDelimited(text: string): string[][] {
  const first = text.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0] ?? ''
  const delim = (first.match(/\t/g)?.length ?? 0) > (first.match(/,/g)?.length ?? 0) ? '\t' : (first.match(/;/g)?.length ?? 0) > (first.match(/,/g)?.length ?? 0) ? ';' : ','
  const rows: string[][] = []
  let row: string[] = [], cell = '', q = false
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text[i]
    if (q) {
      if (c === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; i++ } else q = false }
      else cell += c
    } else if (c === '"') q = true
    else if (c === delim) { row.push(cell); cell = '' }
    else if (c === '\n' || c === '\r') {
      if (c === '\r' && text[i + 1] === '\n') i++
      row.push(cell); cell = ''
      if (row.some((x) => x.trim() !== '')) rows.push(row)
      row = []
    } else cell += c
  }
  row.push(cell)
  if (row.some((x) => x.trim() !== '')) rows.push(row)
  return rows
}

/**
 * Column aliases accepted from common SAP extracts (CJI3 / KSB1 / S/4 ACDOCA exports, English headers). Aliases are
 * tried in order, so when an extract has both a vendor number ("Vendor") and a vendor name ("Name") the name wins.
 */
const COLS: Record<SapColumn, string[]> = {
  sapWbs: ['wbs element', 'wbs', 'object', 'wbs elem', 'posid', 'ps_posid'],
  costElement: ['cost element', 'cost elem', 'gl account', 'g/l account', 'kstar', 'racct', 'account'],
  costElementName: ['cost element name', 'cost element descr', 'cost elem.name', 'account name', 'gl account name'],
  period: ['period', 'fiscal period', 'posting period', 'per', 'poper', 'fiscal year/period'],
  fiscalYear: ['fiscal year', 'fisc. year', 'year', 'gjahr'],
  postingDate: ['posting date', 'pstng date', 'budat', 'date'],
  amount: ['amount', 'value in obj. currency', 'value in obj. crcy', 'val/coarea crcy', 'val.in rep.cur.', 'amount in local currency', 'hsl', 'wkgbtr', 'value'],
  currency: ['object currency', 'obj. currency', 'objectcurrency', 'currency', 'crcy', 'owaer'],
  vendor: ['vendor name', 'name', 'supplier name', 'vendor', 'supplier', 'lifnr'],
  docNo: ['document number', 'doc. no.', 'documentno', 'belnr', 'document', 'ref doc no'],
  refDoc: ['reference document', 'ref. document', 'reference', 'ref. doc. no.', 'xblnr', 'refbn'],
  poNumber: ['purchasing document', 'purchase order', 'po number', 'ebeln'],
  text: ['text', 'name of offsetting account', 'item text', 'description', 'sgtxt'],
}

function parseAmount(v: string): number {
  let t = v.trim().replace(/[$\s]/g, '')
  let neg = false
  if (/^\(.*\)$/.test(t)) { neg = true; t = t.slice(1, -1) }
  if (t.endsWith('-')) { neg = true; t = t.slice(0, -1) } // SAP trailing minus
  if (/^-?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+(,\d+)?$/.test(t)) t = t.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.') // 1.234,56
  else t = t.replace(/,/g, '')
  const n = Number(t)
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? (neg ? -Math.abs(n) : n) : NaN
}

function toPeriod(period: string, postingDate: string, fallback: string, fiscalYear = ''): string {
  const p = period.trim()
  // CJI3 shows the fiscal year and a bare period number (9 or 009) in separate columns. Fiscal year = calendar year
  // here; special periods 13–16 fall back to the posting date.
  // VERIFY: confirm the fiscal-year variant (calendar year, periods 1–12 = Jan–Dec) before reading a real extract.
  if (/^\d{4}$/.test(fiscalYear.trim()) && /^\d{1,3}$/.test(p) && Number(p) >= 1 && Number(p) <= 12) return `${fiscalYear.trim()}-${String(Number(p)).padStart(2, '0')}`
  let m = p.match(/^(\d{4})[-/.](\d{1,3})$/) // 2026-10 or 2026/010
  if (m) return `${m[1]}-${String(Number(m[2])).padStart(2, '0')}`
  m = p.match(/^(\d{1,3})[-/.](\d{4})$/) // 010/2026
  if (m) return `${m[2]}-${String(Number(m[1])).padStart(2, '0')}`
  const d = toISODate(postingDate)
  return d ? d.slice(0, 7) : fallback
}
function toISODate(v: string): string {
  const t = v.trim()
  let m = t.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/)
  if (m) return `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}`
  m = t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[/.](\d{1,2})[/.](\d{4})$/) // US mm/dd/yyyy (SAP US default) or dd.mm.yyyy
  if (m) return t.includes('.') ? `${m[3]}-${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}-${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}` : `${m[3]}-${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}-${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}`
  return ''
}

/** Parse an SAP extract. `fallbackPeriod` is used when neither period nor posting date can be read. */
export function parseSapExtract(text: string, fallbackPeriod: string): { rows: SapRow[]; errors: string[]; columns: Partial<Record<SapColumn, string>> } {
  const grid = readDelimited(text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''))
  const errors: string[] = []
  if (grid.length < 2) return { rows: [], errors: ['The file has no data rows.'], columns: {} }
  const header = grid[0].map((h) => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' '))
  const idx = {} as Record<SapColumn, number>
  const columns: Partial<Record<SapColumn, string>> = {}
  const used = new Set<number>()
  for (const k of Object.keys(COLS) as SapColumn[]) {
    idx[k] = -1
    for (const a of COLS[k]) { const i = header.indexOf(a); if (i >= 0 && !used.has(i)) { idx[k] = i; break } }
    if (idx[k] >= 0) { used.add(idx[k]); columns[k] = grid[0][idx[k]].trim() }
  }
  for (const k of ['sapWbs', 'costElement', 'amount'] as const) if (idx[k] < 0) errors.push(`Missing a "${COLS[k][0]}" column (accepted headers: ${COLS[k].slice(0, 4).join(', ')}).`)
  if (errors.length) return { rows: [], errors, columns }
  const rows: SapRow[] = []
  grid.slice(1).forEach((r, i) => {
    const g = (k: SapColumn) => (idx[k] >= 0 ? (r[idx[k]] ?? '').trim() : '')
    const amount = parseAmount(g('amount'))
    if (!g('sapWbs') && !g('costElement')) return
    if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) { errors.push(`Row ${i + 2}: amount "${g('amount')}" is not a number — skipped.`); return }
    const currency = g('currency').toUpperCase()
    if (currency && currency !== 'USD') { errors.push(`Row ${i + 2}: currency ${currency} — only USD extracts are read; skipped.`); return }
    const postingDate = toISODate(g('postingDate'))
    const row: SapRow = { sapWbs: g('sapWbs'), costElement: g('costElement').replace(/^0+(?=\d{7})/, ''), costElementName: g('costElementName'), period: toPeriod(g('period'), g('postingDate'), fallbackPeriod, g('fiscalYear')), postingDate: postingDate || `${fallbackPeriod}-28`, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100, vendor: g('vendor'), docNo: g('docNo'), text: g('text') }
    if (g('refDoc')) row.refDoc = g('refDoc')
    if (g('poNumber')) row.poNumber = g('poNumber')
    if (currency) row.currency = currency
    rows.push(row)
  })
  return { rows, errors, columns }
}

/** Project for an SAP WBS element: the project whose erpWbs is the element or its parent (C.PSE.1042 / C.PSE.1042.06). */
export function projectForWbs(s: AppState, wbs: string): Project | undefined {
  const w = wbs.trim().toUpperCase()
  return s.projects.filter((p) => w === p.erpWbs.toUpperCase() || w.startsWith(p.erpWbs.toUpperCase() + '.')).sort((a, b) => b.erpWbs.length - a.erpWbs.length)[0]
}

const inRange = (ce: string, from: string, to: string) => {
  const n = Number(ce), a = Number(from), b = Number(to)
  return Number.isFinite(n) && Number.isFinite(a) && Number.isFinite(b) ? n >= a && n <= b : ce >= from && ce <= to
}
/** First active rule (by priority) that matches the row. */
export function matchRule(rules: SapMapping[], r: Pick<SapRow, 'costElement' | 'sapWbs' | 'text' | 'vendor'>): SapMapping | undefined {
  return [...rules].filter((x) => x.active).sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority).find((x) =>
    inRange(r.costElement, x.costElementFrom, x.costElementTo) &&
    (!x.wbsSuffix || r.sapWbs.toUpperCase().endsWith(x.wbsSuffix.toUpperCase())) &&
    (!x.textContains || `${r.text} ${r.vendor}`.toLowerCase().includes(x.textContains.toLowerCase())))
}

/** Classify rows into transactions (no allocation yet — allocation happens per project/period at post time). */
export function classify(s: AppState, rows: SapRow[], importId: string): ActualTxn[] {
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const p = projectForWbs(s, r.sapWbs)
    const rule = matchRule(s.settings.sapMappings, r)
    const base = { id: uid('txn'), importId, projectId: p?.id, period: r.period, postingDate: r.postingDate, sapWbs: r.sapWbs, costElement: r.costElement, costElementName: r.costElementName, docNo: r.docNo, vendor: r.vendor, text: r.text, amount: r.amount, ruleId: rule?.id }
    if (!p) return { ...base, status: 'no-project' as const }
    if (!rule) return { ...base, status: 'unmapped' as const }
    if (rule.target === 'exclude') return { ...base, status: 'excluded' as const }
    if (rule.target === 'allocate') return { ...base, status: 'allocated' as const }
    return { ...base, status: rule.lineId && forecastLines(s).some((l) => l.id === rule.lineId) ? 'mapped' as const : 'unmapped' as const, lineId: rule.lineId }
  })
}

export function summarize(txns: ActualTxn[]) {
  const t = (st: ActualTxn['status'][]) => Math.round(sum(txns.filter((x) => st.includes(x.status)).map((x) => x.amount)))
  return { rows: txns.length, total: t(['mapped', 'allocated', 'unmapped', 'excluded', 'no-project']), mapped: t(['mapped', 'allocated']), unmapped: t(['unmapped']), excluded: t(['excluded']), noProject: t(['no-project']) }
}

/** Stage an import (nothing reaches project actuals until it is posted). Mutates the draft; returns the import. */
export function stageImport(d: AppState, rows: SapRow[], fileName: string, byId = d.currentUserId): ActualsImport {
  const id = uid('imp')
  const txns = classify(d, rows, id)
  const periods = [...new Set(txns.map((t) => t.period))].sort()
  const imp: ActualsImport = { id, fileName, source: 'SAP extract', period: periods[periods.length - 1] ?? d.settings.asOf.slice(0, 7), importedAt: nowISO(), byId, status: 'staged', ...summarize(txns), projectIds: [...new Set(txns.map((t) => t.projectId).filter(Boolean) as string[])] }
  d.actualTxns.push(...txns)
  d.actualsImports.push(imp)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: imp.importedAt, actorId: byId, entity: 'actuals-import', entityId: id, action: 'stage', summary: `Staged SAP extract ${fileName}: ${imp.rows} rows, ${imp.projectIds.length} projects` })
  return imp
}

/** Re-run the mapping table over a staged import (after rules change). Keeps manual resolutions. */
export function remap(d: AppState, importId: string) {
  const imp = d.actualsImports.find((x) => x.id === importId)
  if (!imp || imp.status !== 'staged') return
  for (const t of d.actualTxns.filter((x) => x.importId === importId && !x.manual)) {
    const [n] = classify(d, [t], importId)
    Object.assign(t, { status: n.status, lineId: n.lineId, ruleId: n.ruleId, projectId: n.projectId, allocation: undefined })
  }
  Object.assign(imp, summarize(d.actualTxns.filter((x) => x.importId === importId)))
}

/** Resolve an exception by hand: map to a line, or exclude. */
export function resolveTxn(d: AppState, txnId: string, to: { lineId?: string; exclude?: boolean; projectId?: string }, note: string, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  const t = d.actualTxns.find((x) => x.id === txnId)
  if (!t) return
  if (to.projectId) t.projectId = to.projectId
  if (to.projectId && !to.exclude && !to.lineId && t.status === 'no-project') {
    // project assigned by hand: the row now runs through the mapping table like any other row
    const rule = matchRule(d.settings.sapMappings, t)
    t.ruleId = rule?.id
    t.status = !rule ? 'unmapped' : rule.target === 'exclude' ? 'excluded' : rule.target === 'allocate' ? 'allocated' : rule.lineId && forecastLines(d).some((l) => l.id === rule.lineId) ? 'mapped' : 'unmapped'
    t.lineId = t.status === 'mapped' ? rule?.lineId : undefined
  } else {
    t.status = to.exclude ? 'excluded' : to.lineId ? 'mapped' : t.status
    t.lineId = to.exclude ? undefined : to.lineId ?? t.lineId
  }
  t.manual = { byId, at: nowISO(), note }
  const imp = d.actualsImports.find((x) => x.id === t.importId)
  if (imp) Object.assign(imp, summarize(d.actualTxns.filter((x) => x.importId === t.importId)))
}

/**
 * One project-period's actuals by line from its rows, exactly as posting builds them: mapped rows land on their
 * line, allocated rows (loader settlements) are spread pro-rata over the period's mapped lines (or evenly over every
 * line when nothing is mapped), and the result is rounded to whole dollars with the residual on the largest line so
 * it sums to the rounded total. Other statuses are ignored. `allocations[i]` is row i's split when it was allocated.
 */
export function periodLines(s: AppState, rows: Pick<ActualTxn, 'status' | 'lineId' | 'amount'>[]): { byLine: Record<string, Money>; allocations: (Record<string, Money> | undefined)[] } {
  const byLine: Record<string, Money> = {}
  for (const t of rows) if (t.status === 'mapped' && t.lineId) byLine[t.lineId] = (byLine[t.lineId] || 0) + t.amount
  const direct = Object.keys(byLine).length ? { ...byLine } : Object.fromEntries(forecastLines(s).map((l) => [l.id, 1]))
  const base = sum(Object.values(direct))
  const allocations = rows.map((t) => {
    if (t.status !== 'allocated') return undefined
    const ids = Object.keys(direct)
    const share: Record<string, Money> = {}
    let acc = 0
    ids.forEach((k, i) => { const v = i === ids.length - 1 ? Math.round((t.amount - acc) * 100) / 100 : Math.round(t.amount * (base ? direct[k] / base : 1 / ids.length) * 100) / 100; share[k] = v; acc += v })
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(share)) byLine[k] = (byLine[k] || 0) + v
    return share
  })
  // whole dollars, exact to the rounded total
  const target = Math.round(sum(Object.values(byLine)))
  const whole = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byLine).map(([k, v]) => [k, Math.round(v)]))
  const diff = target - sum(Object.values(whole))
  const big = Object.keys(whole).sort((a, b) => Math.abs(whole[b]) - Math.abs(whole[a]))[0]
  if (big && diff) whole[big] += diff
  return { byLine: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(whole).filter(([, v]) => v !== 0)), allocations }
}

/**
 * Post a staged import. Every (project, period) in the file is rebuilt from SAP: the extract is treated as the
 * complete period-to-date actuals for those projects (re-importing a period replaces it). Allocated rows (loader
 * settlements) are spread over that period's mapped lines pro-rata. Unmapped and no-project rows block posting.
 */
export function postImport(d: AppState, importId: string, byId = d.currentUserId): { ok: boolean; reason?: string; periods: number } {
  const imp = d.actualsImports.find((x) => x.id === importId)
  if (!imp || imp.status !== 'staged') return { ok: false, reason: 'Import is not staged', periods: 0 }
  const txns = d.actualTxns.filter((x) => x.importId === importId)
  const blocking = txns.filter((x) => x.status === 'unmapped' || x.status === 'no-project')
  if (blocking.length) return { ok: false, reason: `${blocking.length} row(s) still need a line or a project`, periods: 0 }
  // supersede earlier posted transactions for the same project-periods
  const keys = new Set(txns.filter((t) => t.projectId).map((t) => `${t.projectId}|${t.period}`))
  const earlier = new Set(d.actualsImports.filter((x) => x.status === 'posted' && x.id !== importId).map((x) => x.id))
  d.actualTxns = d.actualTxns.filter((t) => !(earlier.has(t.importId) && keys.has(`${t.projectId}|${t.period}`)))
  for (const key of keys) {
    const [pid, period] = key.split('|')
    const rows = d.actualTxns.filter((t) => t.importId === importId && t.projectId === pid && t.period === period)
    const { byLine: final, allocations } = periodLines(d, rows)
    rows.forEach((t, i) => { if (allocations[i]) t.allocation = allocations[i] })
    const actual = sum(Object.values(final))
    let cp = d.costPeriods.find((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.period === period)
    if (!cp) {
      cp = { id: uid('cp'), projectId: pid, period, planned: 0, earned: 0, actual: 0, commitments: 0 } as CostPeriod
      d.costPeriods.push(cp)
    }
    cp.actual = actual
    cp.byLine = final
    cp.byFunction = linesToFunctions(d, final)
    cp.source = 'sap'
  }
  imp.status = 'posted'
  imp.postedAt = nowISO()
  imp.postedById = byId
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: imp.postedAt, actorId: byId, entity: 'actuals-import', entityId: importId, action: 'post', summary: `Posted SAP actuals ${imp.fileName}: ${money0(imp.mapped)} to ${keys.size} project-period(s)${imp.excluded ? `, ${money0(imp.excluded)} excluded` : ''}` })
  return { ok: true, periods: keys.size }
}
const money0 = (n: number) => `$${Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US')}`

export function discardImport(d: AppState, importId: string, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  const imp = d.actualsImports.find((x) => x.id === importId)
  if (!imp || imp.status !== 'staged') return
  imp.status = 'discarded'
  d.actualTxns = d.actualTxns.filter((t) => t.importId !== importId)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: nowISO(), actorId: byId, entity: 'actuals-import', entityId: importId, action: 'discard', summary: `Discarded staged SAP extract ${imp.fileName}` })
}

/** Posted SAP transactions for a project (optionally one line and/or period) — the drill-down behind every actual. */
export function txnsFor(s: AppState, pid: string, o: { lineId?: string; fnId?: string; period?: string } = {}): ActualTxn[] {
  const posted = new Set(s.actualsImports.filter((x) => x.status === 'posted').map((x) => x.id))
  return s.actualTxns.filter((t) => posted.has(t.importId) && t.projectId === pid && (!o.period || t.period === o.period) && t.status !== 'excluded' &&
    (!o.lineId || t.lineId === o.lineId || (t.allocation && o.lineId in t.allocation)) &&
    (!o.fnId || (t.lineId && functionOfLine(s, t.lineId) === o.fnId) || (t.allocation && Object.keys(t.allocation).some((l) => functionOfLine(s, l) === o.fnId))))
}

/** Build a realistic SAP extract (CSV) for a period — used for the demo's "download a sample extract" and tests. */
export function sampleExtract(s: AppState, period: string, seed = 1): string {
  let x = seed * 9301 + 49297
  const rnd = () => ((x = (x * 9301 + 49297) % 233280) / 233280)
  const ce = [
    ['5100110', 'Company labor — straight time', 'line'], ['5100120', 'Company labor — overtime', 'line'], ['5100110', 'Company labor — straight time', 'eng'],
    ['5200110', 'Contract services — construction', 'line'], ['5200130', 'Contract services — equipment rental', 'line'], ['5310110', 'Material — line pipe', 'line'],
    ['5320110', 'Material — valves', 'line'], ['5400110', 'Outside engineering services', 'line'], ['5510110', 'Environmental consultants', 'line'],
    ['5600110', 'Inspection services — NDE', 'line'], ['5700110', 'Paving & restoration', 'line'], ['5710110', 'Traffic control services', 'line'],
    ['5800110', 'Project controls services', 'line'], ['5900110', 'A&G overhead settlement', 'alloc'], ['5900120', 'Pension & benefits loader', 'alloc'], ['5900130', 'AFUDC', 'alloc'],
  ] as const
  const vendors = ['Kestrel Ridge Pipeline Co.', 'Larkspur Inspection Co.', 'Cactus Wren Traffic Co.', 'Quillfeather Environmental Co.', 'Bluejay Paving Co.', 'Cobalt Mesa Engineering Co.', 'Ironbark Valve Supply Co.']
  const out: string[] = ['WBS Element,Cost Element,Cost Element Name,Fiscal Period,Posting Date,Amount,Vendor,Document Number,Text']
  const projects = s.projects.filter((p) => p.status === 'active' && p.stageIndex >= 1).slice(0, 8)
  const [y, m] = period.split('-')
  let doc = 4900012000 + seed * 1000
  for (const p of projects) {
    const n = 6 + Math.floor(rnd() * 6)
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const [code, name, kind] = ce[Math.floor(rnd() * ce.length)]
      const amt = Math.round((kind === 'alloc' ? 8000 + rnd() * 40000 : 5000 + rnd() * 180000) * 100) / 100
      const wbs = kind === 'eng' ? `${p.erpWbs}.02` : p.erpWbs
      const day = String(3 + Math.floor(rnd() * 24)).padStart(2, '0')
      out.push([wbs, code, name, `${y}/${String(Number(m)).padStart(3, '0')}`, `${m}/${day}/${y}`, amt.toFixed(2), kind === 'alloc' || code.startsWith('51') ? '' : vendors[Math.floor(rnd() * vendors.length)], String(doc++), kind === 'alloc' ? 'Period-end settlement' : `${p.code} ${name.split(' — ')[0].toLowerCase()}`].map((v) => (/[",]/.test(v) ? `"${v.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : v)).join(','))
    }
  }
  // exceptions to show the workflow: an unknown cost element, a WBS that matches no project, a reversal
  const p0 = projects[0]
  if (p0) {
    out.push([p0.erpWbs, '5450110', 'Software subscriptions', `${y}/${String(Number(m)).padStart(3, '0')}`, `${m}/15/${y}`, '12480.00', 'Mapleleaf Field Software Co.', String(doc++), 'Field data collection licenses'].join(','))
    out.push(['C.XXX.9999', '5200110', 'Contract services — construction', `${y}/${String(Number(m)).padStart(3, '0')}`, `${m}/18/${y}`, '48210.55', 'Kestrel Ridge Pipeline Co.', String(doc++), 'Mis-keyed WBS'].join(','))
    out.push([p0.erpWbs, '5200110', 'Contract services — construction', `${y}/${String(Number(m)).padStart(3, '0')}`, `${m}/20/${y}`, '-15000.00', 'Kestrel Ridge Pipeline Co.', String(doc++), 'Credit memo — retention release reversal'].join(','))
  }
  return out.join('\n')
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/sapSample.ts =====
// The fictional SAP actuals sample committed at samples/sap-actuals-fictional.csv. Everything in it is made up: the
// projects and WBS elements are the seed's fictional "Arroyo Valley Gas" projects, the cost elements are the seed's
// fictional cost elements, and every vendor is a fictional "… Co.". It is laid out like a typical SAP PS line-item
// report (CJI3-style English column headers) so the import reads it the way it would read a real export.
//
// It is built from the seed's posted September (P09) transactions for six projects, so the reconciliation view
// ties for most of them, plus the quirks real extracts have: thousands separators, trailing-minus credits, a
// reversal pair, an unknown cost element, a WBS that matches no project, a balance-sheet row and an ALV total line.
// Regenerate with `npm run sample:sap` (writes the file from this function).
import type { ActualTxn, AppState } from '../types'
import { sum } from '../util'

export const FICTIONAL_SAMPLE_FILE = 'sap-actuals-fictional.csv'
export const FICTIONAL_SAMPLE_PERIOD = '2026-09'

export const CJI3_HEADERS = ['WBS Element', 'Cost Element', 'Cost Element Name', 'Fiscal Year', 'Period', 'Posting Date', 'Document Number', 'Reference Document', 'Purchasing Document', 'Vendor', 'Name', 'Value in Obj. Currency', 'Object Currency', 'Text'] as const

/** 1234.5 → "1,234.50"; −1234.5 → "1,234.50-" (SAP trailing minus). */
export function sapAmount(n: number): string {
  const a = Math.abs(n).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })
  return n < 0 ? `${a}-` : a
}
const csvCell = (v: string) => (/[",\n]/.test(v) ? `"${v.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : v)
const usDate = (iso: string) => `${iso.slice(5, 7)}/${iso.slice(8, 10)}/${iso.slice(0, 4)}`

interface Line { wbs: string; ce: string; ceName: string; date: string; doc: string; ref: string; po: string; vendorNo: string; vendor: string; amount: number; text: string }

/** Build the fictional CJI3-style extract (CSV text). Deterministic for a given seed. */
export function fictionalSapExtract(s: AppState): string {
  const imp = s.actualsImports.find((x) => x.status === 'posted' && x.period === FICTIONAL_SAMPLE_PERIOD)
  const txns = imp ? s.actualTxns.filter((t) => t.importId === imp.id) : []
  // six projects across WBS prefixes (skip the largest program project to keep the sample short)
  const pids = [...new Set(txns.map((t) => t.projectId!).filter(Boolean))]
  const projects = pids.map((id) => s.projects.find((p) => p.id === id)!).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code))
  const byPrefix = new Map<string, typeof projects>()
  for (const p of projects.slice(1)) { const k = p.erpWbs.split('.').slice(0, 2).join('.'); byPrefix.set(k, [...(byPrefix.get(k) ?? []), p]) }
  const pick = [...byPrefix.values()].flatMap((ps) => ps.slice(0, 3)).slice(0, 6).sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code))
  const vendors = [...new Set(txns.map((t) => t.vendor).filter(Boolean))].sort()
  const vName = (v: string) => (v && !/ Co\.$/.test(v) ? `${v.replace(/^City of /, '')} Permit Services Co.` : v)
  const vNo = (v: string) => (v ? String(300010 + vendors.indexOf(v) * 10) : '')
  const lines: Line[] = []
  const from = (t: ActualTxn, p: (typeof pick)[number]): Line => ({
    wbs: t.sapWbs, ce: t.costElement, ceName: t.costElementName, date: t.postingDate, doc: t.docNo,
    ref: t.vendor ? `INV-${t.docNo.slice(-5)}` : '', po: t.vendor ? `45${String(vendors.indexOf(t.vendor) + 1).padStart(2, '0')}0${p.code.replace(/\D/g, '')}` : '',
    vendorNo: vNo(t.vendor), vendor: vName(t.vendor), amount: t.amount, text: t.text,
  })
  for (const p of pick) for (const t of txns.filter((x) => x.projectId === p.id).sort((a, b) => a.sapWbs.localeCompare(b.sapWbs) || a.docNo.localeCompare(b.docNo))) lines.push(from(t, p))

  // quirks
  const [a, b, c] = pick
  const y = FICTIONAL_SAMPLE_PERIOD.slice(0, 4), m = FICTIONAL_SAMPLE_PERIOD.slice(5)
  const d = (day: number) => `${y}-${m}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`
  const q = (x: Partial<Line> & Pick<Line, 'wbs' | 'ce' | 'ceName' | 'doc' | 'amount' | 'text'>): Line => ({ date: d(29), ref: '', po: '', vendorNo: '', vendor: '', ...x })
  if (a) {
    lines.push(q({ wbs: a.erpWbs, ce: '5450110', ceName: 'Software subscriptions', date: d(15), doc: '4900590001', ref: 'INV-77120', vendorNo: '300990', vendor: 'Mapleleaf Field Software Co.', amount: 12_480, text: 'Field data collection licenses' }))
    lines.push(q({ wbs: a.erpWbs, ce: '1400110', ceName: 'Inventory transfer — pipe stock', date: d(21), doc: '4900590002', amount: 86_400, text: 'Pipe transferred from storeroom (balance sheet)' }))
  }
  if (b) {
    lines.push(q({ wbs: b.erpWbs, ce: '5200110', ceName: 'Contract services — construction', date: d(10), doc: '4900590010', ref: 'INV-55012', po: `4599${b.code.replace(/\D/g, '')}`, vendorNo: '300995', vendor: 'Pinecone Builders Co.', amount: 23_450, text: 'Pay app 5 — duplicate entry' }))
    lines.push(q({ wbs: b.erpWbs, ce: '5200110', ceName: 'Contract services — construction', date: d(12), doc: '4900590011', ref: '4900590010', po: `4599${b.code.replace(/\D/g, '')}`, vendorNo: '300995', vendor: 'Pinecone Builders Co.', amount: -23_450, text: 'Reversal of 4900590010 — duplicate invoice' }))
  }
  if (c) lines.push(q({ wbs: c.erpWbs, ce: '5200110', ceName: 'Contract services — construction', date: d(30), doc: '4900590020', ref: 'CM-3301', po: `4598${c.code.replace(/\D/g, '')}`, vendorNo: '300996', vendor: 'Kestrel Ridge Pipeline Co.', amount: -15_000, text: 'Credit memo, backcharge for damaged coating' }))
  lines.push(q({ wbs: 'C.PSE.9999', ce: '5200110', ceName: 'Contract services — construction', date: d(18), doc: '4900590030', ref: 'INV-55107', vendorNo: '300996', vendor: 'Kestrel Ridge Pipeline Co.', amount: 48_210.55, text: 'Pay app 7 — WBS keyed incorrectly' }))

  const out = [CJI3_HEADERS.join(',')]
  for (const l of lines) out.push([l.wbs, l.ce, l.ceName, y, String(Number(m)), usDate(l.date), l.doc, l.ref, l.po, l.vendorNo, l.vendor, sapAmount(l.amount), 'USD', l.text].map(csvCell).join(','))
  // ALV exports end with a total line (no WBS, no cost element) — the import skips it
  out.push(['', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', sapAmount(Math.round(sum(lines.map((l) => l.amount)) * 100) / 100), 'USD', '*'].map(csvCell).join(','))
  return out.join('\n') + '\n'
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/wbs.ts =====
// Forecast spread against the WBS functions. Pure functions; no storage or UI.
import type { AppState, Forecast, Money, Project, WbsFunction } from '../types'
import { addMonths, argmax, monthKey, monthRange, sum } from '../util'
import { activeBudget } from './position'
import { CONT_CAT, ESC_CAT, LOADER_CAT } from './estimates'

export const wbsFunctions = (s: AppState): WbsFunction[] => s.settings.wbsFunctions

/** WBS function a cost category rolls up to (undefined for loaders/escalation/contingency or unmapped). */
export function fnOfCategory(s: AppState, categoryId: string): string | undefined {
  return s.settings.wbsFunctions.find((f) => f.categoryIds.includes(categoryId))?.id
}

/**
 * Split a by-category amount map into WBS functions. Loaders and escalation are allocated pro-rata
 * to the mapped direct cost; contingency is returned separately; unmapped categories are reported.
 */
export function toFunctions(s: AppState, byCategory: Record<string, Money>): { byFn: Record<string, Money>; contingency: Money; unmapped: Money } {
  const byFn: Record<string, Money> = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((f) => [f.id, 0]))
  let pool = 0, contingency = 0, unmapped = 0
  for (const [cat, amt] of Object.entries(byCategory)) {
    if ((cat === LOADER_CAT || cat === ESC_CAT) && !fnOfCategory(s, cat)) { pool += amt; continue } // owned by a function (e.g. 900 Project Overhead) → direct; otherwise pro-rata
    if (cat === CONT_CAT) { contingency += amt; continue }
    const fn = fnOfCategory(s, cat)
    if (fn) byFn[fn] += amt
    else unmapped += amt
  }
  const direct = sum(Object.values(byFn))
  if (pool && direct) {
    const ids = Object.keys(byFn)
    const w = ids.map((id) => byFn[id])
    const adds = w.map((x) => Math.round((pool * x) / direct))
    adds[argmax(w)] += pool - sum(adds)
    ids.forEach((id, i) => { byFn[id] += adds[i] })
  }
  return { byFn, contingency, unmapped }
}

/** Current budget by WBS function (active budget + approved changes), contingency separate. */
export function budgetByFunction(s: AppState, pid: string): { byFn: Record<string, Money>; contingency: Money; total: Money } {
  const b = activeBudget(s, pid)
  const cats: Record<string, Money> = { ...(b?.byCategory ?? {}) }
  for (const c of s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.status === 'approved' && c.fundedFrom === 'new-funding' && (!b || (c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt) >= b.createdAt))) cats[c.categoryId] = (cats[c.categoryId] || 0) + c.costImpact
  const { byFn } = toFunctions(s, cats)
  const contingency = b?.contingency ?? 0
  return { byFn, contingency, total: sum(Object.values(byFn)) + contingency }
}

/** Actuals by function by month. Periods without a split are allocated by budget share. */
export function actualsByFunctionMonth(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, Record<string, Money>> {
  const out: Record<string, Record<string, Money>> = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((f) => [f.id, {}]))
  const bud = budgetByFunction(s, pid).byFn
  const btot = sum(Object.values(bud)) || 1
  for (const cp of s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)) {
    const split = cp.byFunction ?? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(bud).map(([k, v]) => [k, Math.round((cp.actual * v) / btot)]))
    for (const [fn, v] of Object.entries(split)) if (out[fn]) out[fn][cp.period] = (out[fn][cp.period] || 0) + v
  }
  return out
}
export function actualsByFunction(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, Money> {
  const m = actualsByFunctionMonth(s, pid)
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(m).map(([k, v]) => [k, sum(Object.values(v))]))
}

/** Last month with recorded actuals (or the month before as-of when none). */
export function actualsThrough(s: AppState, pid: string): string {
  const ps = s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid).map((c) => c.period).sort()
  return ps[ps.length - 1] ?? monthKey(addMonths(s.settings.asOf, -1))
}

/** Months the forecast can be spread into: the month after actuals through to project finish + 3 months. */
export function forecastMonths(s: AppState, p: Project): string[] {
  const from = monthKey(addMonths(actualsThrough(s, p.id) + '-01', 1))
  const ends = p.milestones.map((m) => m.actual ?? m.forecast).sort()
  const last = ends[ends.length - 1] ?? addMonths(s.settings.asOf, 12)
  const to = monthKey(addMonths(last, 3))
  return to >= from ? monthRange(from, to) : [from]
}

export type SpreadMethod = 'even' | 'front' | 'back' | 'scurve' | 'bell'
export const SPREAD_METHODS: { id: SpreadMethod; label: string; hint: string }[] = [
  { id: 'even', label: 'Even', hint: 'Same amount every month' },
  { id: 'scurve', label: 'S-curve', hint: 'Slow start, peak mid-way, taper' },
  { id: 'front', label: 'Front-loaded', hint: 'Most spend early' },
  { id: 'back', label: 'Back-loaded', hint: 'Most spend late' },
  { id: 'bell', label: 'Bell', hint: 'Concentrated in the middle' },
]

export function curve(method: SpreadMethod, n: number): number[] {
  if (n <= 0) return []
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
    const t = (i + 0.5) / n
    switch (method) {
      case 'even': return 1
      case 'front': return 2 - 1.8 * t
      case 'back': return 0.2 + 1.8 * t
      case 'scurve': return Math.sin(t * Math.PI) + 0.15
      case 'bell': return Math.exp(-(((t - 0.5) / 0.22) ** 2)) + 0.02
    }
  })
}

/** Spread `total` across `months` by a curve; whole dollars that sum exactly to total. */
export function spreadAmount(total: Money, months: string[], method: SpreadMethod): Record<string, Money> {
  const w = curve(method, months.length)
  const W = sum(w) || 1
  const out: Record<string, Money> = {}
  let acc = 0
  months.forEach((m, i) => {
    const v = i === months.length - 1 ? total - acc : Math.round((total * w[i]) / W)
    out[m] = v
    acc += v
  })
  return out
}

const TIMING: Record<WbsFunction['timing'], SpreadMethod> = { early: 'front', mid: 'scurve', late: 'back', flat: 'even' }

/**
 * Default ETC spread: each function's remaining budget (budget − actuals, never negative), scaled so the
 * functions sum to `etcTotal` when given, spread by the function's typical timing.
 */
export function defaultSpread(s: AppState, p: Project, etcTotal?: Money, months = forecastMonths(s, p)): Record<string, Record<string, Money>> {
  const bud = budgetByFunction(s, p.id).byFn
  const act = actualsByFunction(s, p.id)
  const remaining = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((f) => [f.id, Math.max(0, (bud[f.id] || 0) - (act[f.id] || 0))]))
  const rtot = sum(Object.values(remaining))
  const target = etcTotal ?? rtot
  const fns = s.settings.wbsFunctions
  const w = fns.map((f) => (rtot ? remaining[f.id] : 1))
  const amts = w.map((x) => Math.round((target * x) / (sum(w) || 1)))
  amts[argmax(w)] += target - sum(amts)
  const out: Record<string, Record<string, Money>> = {}
  fns.forEach((f, i) => { out[f.id] = spreadAmount(Math.max(0, amts[i]), months, TIMING[f.timing]) })
  return out
}

export const spreadTotal = (spread?: Record<string, Record<string, Money>>) => sum(Object.values(spread ?? {}).flatMap((m) => Object.values(m)))
export const spreadByFunction = (spread?: Record<string, Record<string, Money>>) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(spread ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, sum(Object.values(v))]))
export function spreadByMonth(spread?: Record<string, Record<string, Money>>): Record<string, Money> {
  const out: Record<string, Money> = {}
  for (const m of Object.values(spread ?? {})) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m)) out[k] = (out[k] || 0) + v
  return out
}
export function spreadByYear(spread?: Record<string, Record<string, Money>>): Record<string, Money> {
  const out: Record<string, Money> = {}
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(spreadByMonth(spread))) out[k.slice(0, 4)] = (out[k.slice(0, 4)] || 0) + v
  return out
}

/** Latest forecast version for a project (any status). */
export const latestForecast = (s: AppState, pid: string): Forecast | undefined =>
  s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || (a.status === 'draft' ? 1 : -1)).pop()

/** EAC implied by a spread: actuals to date + ETC + contingency held. */
export function eacOf(s: AppState, pid: string, spread?: Record<string, Record<string, Money>>, contingencyHeld = 0): Money {
  const act = sum(s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid).map((c) => c.actual))
  return act + spreadTotal(spread) + contingencyHeld
}

/** Function-level view used by the forecast grid and portfolio roll-ups. */
export interface FnRow { fn: WbsFunction; budget: Money; actual: Money; etc: Money; eac: Money; variance: Money }
export function functionRows(s: AppState, p: Project, f?: Forecast): FnRow[] {
  const bud = budgetByFunction(s, p.id).byFn
  const act = actualsByFunction(s, p.id)
  const etc = spreadByFunction(f?.spread)
  return s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((fn) => {
    const e = (act[fn.id] || 0) + (etc[fn.id] || 0)
    return { fn, budget: bud[fn.id] || 0, actual: act[fn.id] || 0, etc: etc[fn.id] || 0, eac: e, variance: (bud[fn.id] || 0) - e }
  })
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/workflow.ts =====
// Workflow engine (Module 7): routes a record through a configured process definition.
import type { AppState, Assignee, Person, RecordType, WfEdge, WfNode, WorkflowDef, WorkflowInstance } from '../types'
import { addDays, evalCondition, nowISO, uid } from '../util'
import { approveEstimate } from './estimates'
import { takeSnapshot } from './position'
import { decideGate } from './gates'
import { publishModel, returnModel } from './factorModel'

export const latestDef = (s: AppState, key: string): WorkflowDef | undefined =>
  s.workflowDefs.filter((d) => d.key === key && d.status === 'published').sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)[0]

/** People who must act on a node for this instance. Out-of-office approvers are replaced by their delegate. */
export function resolveAssignees(s: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance, a?: Assignee): string[] {
  if (!a) return []
  const project = inst.projectId ? s.projects.find((p) => p.id === inst.projectId) : undefined
  let ids: string[] = []
  switch (a.kind) {
    case 'person': ids = [a.personId]; break
    case 'requester': ids = [inst.startedById]; break
    case 'role': {
      ids = s.people.filter((p) => p.roles.includes(a.roleId)).map((p) => p.id)
      break
    }
    case 'team-position': {
      const id = project?.team[a.position]
      ids = id ? [id] : []
      break
    }
    case 'manager-of-requester': {
      const m = s.people.find((p) => p.id === inst.startedById)?.managerId
      ids = m ? [m] : []
      break
    }
    case 'doa': {
      const amount = Math.abs(Number(inst.data[a.field] ?? 0))
      const role = doaRoleFor(s, inst, amount)
      ids = role ? s.people.filter((p) => p.roles.includes(role) && !p.company).map((p) => p.id) : []
      break
    }
  }
  return Array.from(new Set(ids.map((id) => delegateFor(s, id))))
}

export function doaRoleFor(s: AppState, inst: Pick<WorkflowInstance, 'projectId'>, amount: number) {
  const project = inst.projectId ? s.projects.find((p) => p.id === inst.projectId) : undefined
  const prog = project ? s.programs.find((p) => p.id === project.programId) : undefined
  const m = s.doaMatrices.find((x) => x.id === prog?.doaMatrixId) ?? s.doaMatrices[0]
  if (!m) return undefined
  const rows = m.rows.slice().sort((a, b) => a.upTo - b.upTo)
  return (rows.find((r) => amount <= r.upTo) ?? rows[rows.length - 1]).roleId
}

function delegateFor(s: AppState, id: string): string {
  return activeDelegate(s, id) ?? id
}

/** The person covering for `id` today (out-of-office window includes settings.asOf), if any. */
export function activeDelegate(s: Pick<AppState, 'people' | 'settings'>, id: string): string | undefined {
  const oo = s.people.find((x) => x.id === id)?.outOfOffice
  return oo && s.settings.asOf >= oo.from && s.settings.asOf <= oo.to && oo.delegateId !== id ? oo.delegateId : undefined
}

/** Assignee on this slot that `actorId` may act for as an out-of-office delegate (steps assigned before the absence). */
export function coveredAssignee(s: Pick<AppState, 'people' | 'settings'>, slot: WorkflowInstance['active'][number], actorId: string): string | undefined {
  if (slot.assigneeIds.includes(actorId)) return undefined
  return slot.assigneeIds.find((x) => !slot.actedIds.includes(x) && activeDelegate(s, x) === actorId)
}

const outgoing = (def: WorkflowDef, nodeId: string) => def.edges.filter((e) => e.from === nodeId)
const nodeOf = (def: WorkflowDef, id: string) => def.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id)!
const defOf = (s: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance) => s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === inst.defId)!

function event(inst: WorkflowInstance, nodeId: string, actorId: string, action: WorkflowInstance['history'][number]['action'], comment?: string) {
  inst.history.push({ id: uid('ev'), at: nowISO(), nodeId, actorId, action, comment, signature: action === 'approve' ? { method: 'password-reauth', hash: Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 14) } : undefined })
}

/** Enter a node: create the active task, or evaluate/pass through automatic nodes. */
function enter(d: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance, def: WorkflowDef, nodeId: string, guard = 0) {
  if (guard > 50) throw new Error('Workflow loop detected')
  const n = nodeOf(def, nodeId)
  const follow = (edges: WfEdge[]) => edges.forEach((e) => enter(d, inst, def, e.to, guard + 1))
  switch (n.type) {
    case 'start':
      follow(outgoing(def, n.id).slice(0, 1))
      return
    case 'form':
    case 'review':
    case 'approval': {
      const assigneeIds = resolveAssignees(d, inst, n.assignee)
      if (!assigneeIds.length) {
        event(inst, n.id, 'system', 'system', 'No assignee resolved; step skipped')
        follow(outgoing(def, n.id).filter((e) => !e.outcome || e.outcome === 'approve' || e.outcome === 'default').slice(0, 1))
        return
      }
      inst.active.push({ nodeId: n.id, since: nowISO(), assigneeIds, actedIds: [], due: n.slaDays ? addDays(d.settings.asOf, n.slaDays) : undefined })
      for (const id of assigneeIds) notify(d, id, `${n.label}: ${inst.title}`, `inbox`)
      return
    }
    case 'condition': {
      const edges = outgoing(def, n.id)
      const ctx = { ...inst.data, project: inst.projectId ? d.projects.find((p) => p.id === inst.projectId) : undefined }
      const hit = edges.find((e) => e.condition && evalCondition(e.condition, ctx)) ?? edges.find((e) => !e.condition)
      event(inst, n.id, 'system', 'system', `Condition → ${hit?.label ?? 'default'}`)
      if (hit) enter(d, inst, def, hit.to, guard + 1)
      return
    }
    case 'parallel-split':
      follow(outgoing(def, n.id))
      return
    case 'parallel-join': {
      const key = `__join_${n.id}`
      const arrived = Number(inst.data[key] ?? 0) + 1
      const needed = def.edges.filter((e) => e.to === n.id).length
      inst.data[key] = arrived
      if (arrived >= needed) {
        inst.data[key] = 0
        follow(outgoing(def, n.id).slice(0, 1))
      }
      return
    }
    case 'notify': {
      for (const id of resolveAssignees(d, inst, n.assignee)) notify(d, id, `${n.label}: ${inst.title}`, inst.projectId ? `project` : 'inbox')
      event(inst, n.id, 'system', 'system', n.label)
      follow(outgoing(def, n.id).slice(0, 1))
      return
    }
    case 'system':
      event(inst, n.id, 'system', 'system', n.label)
      follow(outgoing(def, n.id).slice(0, 1))
      return
    case 'end':
      complete(d, inst, (inst.data.__outcome as WorkflowInstance['status']) ?? 'approved')
      return
  }
}

function notify(d: AppState, toId: string, text: string, link: string) {
  d.notifications.push({ id: uid('ntf'), at: nowISO(), toId, text, link, read: false })
}

export interface StartArgs { title: string; recordType: RecordType; recordId: string; projectId?: string; data?: Record<string, unknown>; startedById?: string }

/** Start a process by key. Returns the instance (or undefined if no published definition). */
export function startWorkflow(d: AppState, key: string, a: StartArgs): WorkflowInstance | undefined {
  const def = latestDef(d, key)
  if (!def) return
  const inst: WorkflowInstance = {
    id: uid('wf'),
    defId: def.id,
    defKey: def.key,
    defVersion: def.version,
    title: a.title,
    recordType: a.recordType,
    recordId: a.recordId,
    projectId: a.projectId,
    status: 'running',
    active: [],
    startedAt: nowISO(),
    startedById: a.startedById ?? d.currentUserId,
    data: { ...(a.data ?? {}) },
    history: [],
  }
  d.workflows.push(inst)
  event(inst, def.nodes.find((n) => n.type === 'start')!.id, inst.startedById, 'start')
  onStart(d, inst)
  enter(d, inst, def, def.nodes.find((n) => n.type === 'start')!.id)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: nowISO(), actorId: inst.startedById, entity: 'workflow', entityId: inst.id, projectId: inst.projectId, action: 'start', summary: `Started ${def.name}: ${inst.title}` })
  return inst
}

export type Decision = 'approve' | 'reject' | 'return' | 'submit'

/** An assignee acts on an active step (WF-04, WF-07, WF-10). */
/** Processes where the submitter may never be the approver. */
export const SEPARATE_DUTIES = new Set(['factor-model-publish'])

export function actOnWorkflow(d: AppState, instId: string, nodeId: string, actorId: string, decision: Decision, comment?: string) {
  const inst = d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === instId)
  if (!inst || inst.status !== 'running') return
  const def = defOf(d, inst)
  const n = nodeOf(def, nodeId)
  const slot = inst.active.find((a) => a.nodeId === nodeId)
  if (!slot) return
  const actor = d.people.find((p) => p.id === actorId)
  const onBehalf = coveredAssignee(d, slot, actorId)
  const allowed = slot.assigneeIds.includes(actorId) || !!onBehalf || actor?.roles.includes('admin')
  if (!allowed) throw new Error('You are not an assignee on this step')
  // Separation of duties: whoever submitted a factor model cannot approve it (not even as an admin).
  if (decision === 'approve' && SEPARATE_DUTIES.has(inst.defKey) && actorId === inst.startedById) throw new Error('The person who submitted this cannot approve it')
  if (onBehalf) {
    const who = d.people.find((p) => p.id === onBehalf)?.name ?? onBehalf
    comment = `On behalf of ${who} (out of office)${comment ? ` — ${comment}` : ''}`
  }
  event(inst, nodeId, actorId, decision === 'submit' ? 'submit' : decision, comment)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: nowISO(), actorId, entity: 'workflow', entityId: inst.id, projectId: inst.projectId, action: decision, summary: `${n.label}: ${decision}${comment ? ` — ${comment}` : ''}` })
  if (decision === 'approve' || decision === 'submit') {
    slot.actedIds.push(actorId)
    if (onBehalf) slot.actedIds.push(onBehalf)
    if (n.rule === 'all' && !slot.assigneeIds.every((id) => slot.actedIds.includes(id))) return
    inst.active = inst.active.filter((a) => a !== slot)
    const edges = outgoing(def, nodeId)
    const next = edges.filter((e) => e.outcome === 'approve' || e.outcome === 'default' || !e.outcome)
    next.forEach((e) => enter(d, inst, def, e.to))
    return
  }
  inst.active = inst.active.filter((a) => a !== slot)
  const edge = outgoing(def, nodeId).find((e) => e.outcome === decision)
  if (edge) {
    enter(d, inst, def, edge.to)
    return
  }
  inst.active = []
  complete(d, inst, decision === 'reject' ? 'rejected' : 'returned')
}

export function recallWorkflow(d: AppState, instId: string, actorId: string) {
  const inst = d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === instId)
  if (!inst || inst.status !== 'running') return
  event(inst, inst.active[0]?.nodeId ?? '', actorId, 'recall')
  inst.active = []
  complete(d, inst, 'cancelled')
}

function complete(d: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance, status: WorkflowInstance['status']) {
  if (inst.status !== 'running') return
  inst.status = status
  inst.completedAt = nowISO()
  inst.active = []
  event(inst, '', 'system', 'complete', status)
  onComplete(d, inst)
  notify(d, inst.startedById, `${inst.title} — ${status}`, 'inbox')
}

const lastApprover = (inst: WorkflowInstance) => [...inst.history].reverse().find((h) => h.action === 'approve')?.actorId ?? inst.startedById

/** Record status while in workflow. */
function onStart(d: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance) {
  const id = inst.recordId
  switch (inst.recordType) {
    case 'estimate': { const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === id); if (e) { e.status = 'in-review'; e.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'change': { const c = d.changes.find((x) => x.id === id); if (c) { c.status = 'in-review'; c.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'safety-plan': { const p = d.safetyPlans.find((x) => x.id === id); if (p) { p.status = 'in-review'; p.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'variance': { const v = d.variances.find((x) => x.id === id); if (v) { v.status = 'submitted'; v.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'deliverable': { const v = d.deliverables.find((x) => x.id === id); if (v) { v.status = 'submitted'; v.updatedAt = nowISO() } break }
    case 'contingency': { const v = d.contingencyDraws.find((x) => x.id === id); if (v) { v.status = 'in-review'; v.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'contract': { const v = d.contracts.find((x) => x.id === id); if (v) v.workflowInstanceId = inst.id; break }
    case 'gate': { const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === inst.projectId); if (p) inst.data.__stage = p.stageIndex; break }
    case 'forecast': { const f = d.forecasts.find((x) => x.id === id); if (f) { f.status = 'submitted'; f.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'commitment-change': { const c = d.commitmentChanges.find((x) => x.id === id); if (c) { c.status = 'in-review'; c.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
    case 'factor-model': { const m = d.factorModels.find((x) => x.id === id); if (m) { m.status = 'in-review'; m.workflowInstanceId = inst.id } break }
  }
}

/** Apply the outcome to the underlying record (completion hooks). */
function onComplete(d: AppState, inst: WorkflowInstance) {
  const ok = inst.status === 'approved'
  const id = inst.recordId
  switch (inst.recordType) {
    case 'estimate': {
      const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (!e) break
      if (ok) approveEstimate(d, id, lastApprover(inst))
      else e.status = inst.status === 'rejected' ? 'rejected' : 'draft'
      break
    }
    case 'change': {
      const c = d.changes.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (!c) break
      if (!ok) { c.status = inst.status === 'rejected' ? 'rejected' : 'draft'; break }
      c.status = 'approved'
      c.decidedAt = d.settings.asOf
      const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === c.projectId)
      if (p && c.scheduleImpactDays) for (const m of p.milestones) if (!m.actual && m.forecast >= d.settings.asOf) m.forecast = addDays(m.forecast, c.scheduleImpactDays)
      const snap = takeSnapshot(d, c.projectId, 'current', `Current baseline after ${c.code}`, { changeId: c.id })
      c.resultingSnapshotId = snap.id
      break
    }
    case 'safety-plan': {
      const sp = d.safetyPlans.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (!sp) break
      if (ok) {
        for (const o of d.safetyPlans) if (o.projectId === sp.projectId && o.workPackage === sp.workPackage && o.status === 'approved' && o.id !== sp.id) o.status = 'superseded'
        sp.status = 'approved'
        sp.approvedAt = d.settings.asOf
      } else sp.status = 'draft'
      break
    }
    case 'contingency': { const x = d.contingencyDraws.find((v) => v.id === id); if (x) x.status = ok ? 'approved' : 'rejected'; break }
    case 'variance': { const x = d.variances.find((v) => v.id === id); if (x) x.status = ok ? 'accepted' : 'returned'; break }
    case 'deliverable': { const x = d.deliverables.find((v) => v.id === id); if (x) { x.status = ok ? 'accepted' : 'returned'; x.updatedAt = nowISO() } break }
    case 'waiver': {
      const x = d.deliverables.find((v) => v.id === inst.data.deliverableId)
      if (x && ok) { x.status = 'waived'; x.waiverReason = String(inst.data.reason ?? '') }
      break
    }
    case 'contract': { const x = d.contracts.find((v) => v.id === id); if (x && ok) { x.status = 'awarded'; x.awarded = d.settings.asOf } break }
    case 'budget': { const b = d.budgets.find((x) => x.id === id); if (b && ok) b.postedToErp = true; break }
    case 'forecast': { const f = d.forecasts.find((x) => x.id === id); if (f) { f.status = ok ? 'approved' : 'returned'; f.locked = ok } break }
    case 'commitment-change': {
      const c = d.commitmentChanges.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (!c) break
      c.status = ok ? 'approved' : inst.status === 'rejected' ? 'rejected' : 'draft'
      c.decidedAt = d.settings.asOf
      if (ok) { const k = d.contracts.find((x) => x.id === c.contractId); if (k) k.changeOrders += c.amount }
      break
    }
    case 'factor-model': {
      if (ok) publishModel(d, id, lastApprover(inst))
      else returnModel(d, id, lastApprover(inst))
      break
    }
    case 'gate': {
      // Board approval through the routed workflow records the gate decision (same effect as the Lifecycle panel).
      const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === inst.projectId)
      if (!ok || !p || p.status !== 'active' || inst.data.__stage !== p.stageIndex) break
      const rec = String(inst.data.recommendation ?? '').toLowerCase()
      decideGate(d, p.id, rec.includes('condition') ? 'go-with-conditions' : 'go', [lastApprover(inst)], 'Approved through the gate-review workflow')
      break
    }
  }
}

export interface Task { inst: WorkflowInstance; node: WfNode; def: WorkflowDef; since: string; due?: string; overdue: boolean; actingFor?: string }

/** Inbox: steps waiting on this person, plus steps assigned to anyone they are covering for while that person is out of office. */
export function tasksFor(s: AppState, personId: string): Task[] {
  const out: Task[] = []
  for (const inst of s.workflows) {
    if (inst.status !== 'running') continue
    const def = s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === inst.defId)
    if (!def) continue
    for (const a of inst.active) {
      if (a.assigneeIds.includes(personId) && !a.actedIds.includes(personId)) {
        out.push({ inst, node: nodeOf(def, a.nodeId), def, since: a.since, due: a.due, overdue: !!a.due && a.due < s.settings.asOf })
      } else {
        const actingFor = coveredAssignee(s, a, personId)
        if (actingFor) out.push({ inst, node: nodeOf(def, a.nodeId), def, since: a.since, due: a.due, overdue: !!a.due && a.due < s.settings.asOf, actingFor })
      }
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => (a.due ?? '9').localeCompare(b.due ?? '9'))
}

/** Dry-run routing for the designer's test mode (WF-09): who would this reach, step by step? */
export function simulate(s: AppState, def: WorkflowDef, data: Record<string, unknown>, projectId?: string, startedById?: string): { node: WfNode; people: Person[] }[] {
  const fake: WorkflowInstance = { id: 'sim', defId: def.id, defKey: def.key, defVersion: def.version, title: 'Simulation', recordType: def.recordType, recordId: 'sim', projectId, status: 'running', active: [], startedAt: nowISO(), startedById: startedById ?? s.currentUserId, data, history: [] }
  const out: { node: WfNode; people: Person[] }[] = []
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  const walk = (id: string, depth: number) => {
    if (depth > 40) return
    const n = def.nodes.find((x) => x.id === id)
    if (!n) return
    if (seen.has(id) && n.type !== 'parallel-join') return
    seen.add(id)
    if (n.type === 'approval' || n.type === 'review' || n.type === 'form' || n.type === 'notify') {
      out.push({ node: n, people: resolveAssignees(s, fake, n.assignee).map((pid) => s.people.find((p) => p.id === pid)!).filter(Boolean) })
    }
    const edges = def.edges.filter((e) => e.from === id)
    if (n.type === 'condition') {
      const ctx = { ...data, project: projectId ? s.projects.find((p) => p.id === projectId) : undefined }
      const hit = edges.find((e) => e.condition && evalCondition(e.condition, ctx)) ?? edges.find((e) => !e.condition)
      if (hit) walk(hit.to, depth + 1)
    } else if (n.type === 'parallel-split') edges.forEach((e) => walk(e.to, depth + 1))
    else {
      const e = edges.find((e) => !e.outcome || e.outcome === 'approve' || e.outcome === 'default')
      if (e) walk(e.to, depth + 1)
    }
  }
  walk(def.nodes.find((n) => n.type === 'start')!.id, 0)
  return out
}

/** Swimlane label for a node (documentation export). */
export function laneOf(s: AppState, n: WfNode): string {
  if (n.lane) return n.lane
  const a = n.assignee
  if (!a) return 'System'
  switch (a.kind) {
    case 'role': return s.roles.find((r) => r.id === a.roleId)?.name ?? a.roleId
    case 'team-position': return ({ pm: 'Project Manager', sponsor: 'Project Sponsor', projectControls: 'Project Controls', constructionManager: 'Construction Manager', safety: 'Safety Advisor', estimator: 'Estimator', engineer: 'Engineer', scheduler: 'Scheduler', environmental: 'Environmental', land: 'Land' } as Record<string, string>)[a.position] ?? a.position
    case 'doa': return 'Approver by dollar authority'
    case 'manager-of-requester': return "Requester's manager"
    case 'requester': return 'Requester'
    case 'person': return s.people.find((p) => p.id === a.personId)?.name ?? 'Person'
  }
}

===== FILE: src/core/ai.ts =====
// Optional AI assist. Off by default; an administrator points it at the organization's approved LLM
// gateway (any OpenAI-compatible /chat/completions endpoint, e.g. Azure OpenAI behind the API gateway)
// in Configuration → AI assist. When it is off, every AI control is hidden and nothing leaves the page.
// AI only drafts; people review and accept. Every call must come from a user click.
//   const text = await ask(prompt, { onText: t => setDraft(t) })
//   const data = await askJSON<{ risks: … }>(prompt)
import { getState, useStore } from './store'

export class AiError extends Error { constructor(public code: string, message: string, public partial?: string) { super(message) } }

export const AI_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  unavailable: 'AI assist is turned off. An administrator can connect it in Configuration → AI assist.',
  http: 'The AI service returned an error. Check the endpoint in Configuration → AI assist.',
  rate_limited: 'Too many AI requests at once. Wait a moment and try again.',
  cancelled: 'Stopped.',
}
export const aiMessage = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof AiError ? (e.code === 'http' ? `${AI_MESSAGES.http} (${e.message})` : AI_MESSAGES[e.code] ?? e.message) : 'Something went wrong drafting this. Try again.')

type Msg = { role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool'; content: string | null; tool_calls?: any[]; tool_call_id?: string }
export interface AiTool { name: string; description: string; inputSchema?: Record<string, unknown>; execute: (input: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown }
export interface AskOptions {
  onText?: (text: string) => void
  signal?: AbortSignal
  tier?: 'quick' | 'default' | 'complex'
  tools?: AiTool[]
  cache?: boolean
}

function config() {
  const a = getState().settings.ai
  if (!a?.enabled || !a.endpoint) throw new AiError('unavailable', AI_MESSAGES.unavailable)
  return a
}

async function complete(messages: Msg[], o: AskOptions, json = false): Promise<Msg> {
  const a = config()
  const body: Record<string, unknown> = { model: a.model || undefined, messages, temperature: 0.2 }
  if (o.tools?.length) body.tools = o.tools.map((t) => ({ type: 'function', function: { name: t.name, description: t.description, parameters: t.inputSchema ?? { type: 'object', properties: {} } } }))
  if (json) body.response_format = { type: 'json_object' }
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  if (a.apiKey) { headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${a.apiKey}`; headers['api-key'] = a.apiKey }
  let r: Response
  try { r = await fetch(a.endpoint, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: o.signal }) }
  catch (e: any) { throw new AiError(e?.name === 'AbortError' ? 'cancelled' : 'http', e?.message ?? 'Network error') }
  if (r.status === 429) throw new AiError('rate_limited', AI_MESSAGES.rate_limited)
  if (!r.ok) throw new AiError('http', `${r.status} ${r.statusText}`)
  const j = await r.json()
  const m = j?.choices?.[0]?.message
  if (!m) throw new AiError('http', 'Empty response')
  return m as Msg
}

export async function ask(input: string | { role: 'user' | 'assistant'; content: string }[], o: AskOptions = {}): Promise<string> {
  const messages: Msg[] = typeof input === 'string' ? [{ role: 'user', content: input }] : input.map((m) => ({ ...m }))
  for (let turn = 0; turn < 6; turn++) {
    const m = await complete(messages, o)
    if (m.tool_calls?.length && o.tools?.length) {
      messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: m.content ?? null, tool_calls: m.tool_calls })
      for (const c of m.tool_calls) {
        const tool = o.tools.find((t) => t.name === c.function?.name)
        let out: unknown
        try { out = tool ? await tool.execute(JSON.parse(c.function?.arguments || '{}')) : { error: 'unknown tool' } } catch (e: any) { out = { error: e?.message ?? 'tool failed' } }
        messages.push({ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: c.id, content: JSON.stringify(out).slice(0, 60_000) })
      }
      continue
    }
    const text = m.content ?? ''
    o.onText?.(text)
    return text
  }
  throw new AiError('http', 'Too many tool steps')
}

export async function askJSON<T>(prompt: string, o: Omit<AskOptions, 'onText'> = {}): Promise<T> {
  const m = await complete([{ role: 'system', content: 'Reply with a single JSON object only, no prose.' }, { role: 'user', content: prompt }], o, true)
  const raw = (m.content ?? '').replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*|\s*```$/g, '')
  try { return JSON.parse(raw) as T } catch { throw new AiError('http', 'The AI reply was not valid JSON') }
}

/** true when an administrator has turned AI assist on and configured an endpoint. */
export function useAiAvailable(): boolean {
  return useStore((s) => !!(s.settings.ai?.enabled && s.settings.ai.endpoint))
}

===== FILE: src/core/auth/index.ts =====
// Login through a swappable OIDC interface (AGENT_RULES rule 20). The app uses only the user's ID, name and
// group claims; groups map to Crosswalk roles. The demo uses the persona switcher (no login, fictional people).
// A production OIDC provider (e.g. the company identity provider) implements the same interface — not built yet.
import type { Person, RoleId } from '../types'

export interface AuthUser { id: string; name: string; groups: string[] }

export interface AuthProvider {
  readonly kind: 'demo' | 'oidc'
  /** The signed-in user, or null when nobody is signed in. */
  currentUser(): AuthUser | null
  signIn(): Promise<void>
  signOut(): Promise<void>
}

/** Group claim → Crosswalk roles. Configured per deployment; the demo maps `crosswalk-<role>` to `<role>`. */
export type GroupRoleMap = Record<string, RoleId[]>

export function rolesForGroups(map: GroupRoleMap, groups: string[]): RoleId[] {
  return Array.from(new Set(groups.flatMap((g) => map[g] ?? [])))
}

export const demoGroupMap = (roles: RoleId[]): GroupRoleMap => Object.fromEntries(roles.map((r) => [`crosswalk-${r}`, [r]]))

/** Demo provider: the persona switcher picks one of the fictional people; their roles become group claims. */
export function demoAuthProvider(getPerson: () => Person | undefined): AuthProvider {
  return {
    kind: 'demo',
    currentUser() {
      const p = getPerson()
      return p ? { id: p.id, name: p.name, groups: p.roles.map((r) => `crosswalk-${r}`) } : null
    },
    async signIn() { /* demo: nothing to do */ },
    async signOut() { /* demo: nothing to do */ },
  }
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/index.ts =====
// The one data-access instance the app uses (rule 17): the shared Postgres-backed API. Swap the
// implementation here without touching any screen or the engine.
import { sharedDataAccess } from './server'
import type { DataAccess } from './types'

export type { DataAccess, RemoteChange, RemoteState, CommitResult } from './types'
export { gzipBase64, gunzipBase64 } from './server'
export const data: DataAccess = sharedDataAccess()

/** UI preferences through the data-access interface (kept in this browser only). */
export const prefs = {
  getRaw: (key: string) => data.getPref(key),
  setRaw: (key: string, value: string) => data.setPref(key, value),
  remove: (key: string) => data.removePref(key),
  get<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
    const v = data.getPref(key)
    if (v == null) return fallback
    try { return JSON.parse(v) as T } catch { return fallback }
  },
  set(key: string, value: unknown) { data.setPref(key, JSON.stringify(value)) },
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/server.ts =====
// Shared implementation of the data-access interface: the app's own API (src/server/api.ts) over relative
// URLs, so it works wherever the page is served from. The only network calls this app makes for data.
import type { CommitResult, DataAccess, RemoteChange } from './types'

function storage(): Storage | null {
  try { return (globalThis as { localStorage?: Storage }).localStorage ?? null } catch { return null }
}

async function call(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}, passcode?: string): Promise<Response | null> {
  try {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'content-type': 'application/json' }
    if (passcode) headers['x-crosswalk-passcode'] = passcode
    return await fetch('api/' + path, { ...init, headers, cache: 'no-store' })
  } catch { return null }
}
const ok = async <T,>(r: Response | null): Promise<T | null> => (r && r.ok ? ((await r.json()) as T) : null)

export function sharedDataAccess(): DataAccess {
  const mem = new Map<string, string>()
  return {
    kind: 'shared',
    async health() {
      const r = await ok<{ ok: boolean; editable: boolean }>(await call('health'))
      return r?.ok ? { editable: !!r.editable } : null
    },
    async auth(passcode) {
      const r = await call('auth', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }, passcode)
      return !!r && r.status === 204
    },
    async loadState(schemaVersion) {
      const r = await ok<{ generation: { id: number } | null; snapshot: { seq: number; stateGz: string } | null; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }>(await call(`state?schema=${schemaVersion}`))
      if (!r) throw new Error('Could not load the shared data')
      return { generation: r.generation?.id ?? null, snapshotGz: r.snapshot?.stateGz ?? null, snapshotSeq: r.snapshot?.seq ?? 0, changes: r.changes, head: r.head }
    },
    async changesSince(schemaVersion, generation, after) {
      const r = await ok<{ reset?: boolean; changes?: RemoteChange[]; head?: number }>(await call(`changes?schema=${schemaVersion}&generation=${generation}&after=${after}`))
      if (!r) throw new Error('Could not reach the shared data')
      return r.reset ? { reset: true } : { reset: false, changes: r.changes ?? [], head: r.head ?? after }
    },
    async appendChange(passcode, c): Promise<CommitResult> {
      const r = await call('changes', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(c) }, passcode)
      if (!r) return 'error'
      if (r.ok) return 'ok'
      if (r.status === 409) return 'conflict'
      if (r.status === 401 || r.status === 403) return 'unauthorized'
      return 'error'
    },
    async addSnapshot(passcode, generation, seq, stateGz) {
      const r = await call('snapshot', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ generation, seq, stateGz }) }, passcode)
      return !!r && r.ok
    },
    async startGeneration(passcode, schema, reason, actor, stateGz) {
      const r = await ok<{ generation: { id: number } }>(await call('generation', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ schema, reason, actor, stateGz }) }, passcode))
      return r?.generation.id ?? null
    },
    getPref(key) {
      try { const v = storage()?.getItem(key); if (v != null) return v } catch { /* blocked */ }
      return mem.get(key) ?? null
    },
    setPref(key, value) {
      mem.set(key, value)
      try { storage()?.setItem(key, value) } catch { /* blocked */ }
    },
    removePref(key) {
      mem.delete(key)
      try { storage()?.removeItem(key) } catch { /* blocked */ }
    },
  }
}

/** gzip + base64, so a ~3.5 MB state travels and is stored as a few hundred KB of text. */
export async function gzipBase64(text: string): Promise<string> {
  const stream = new Blob([text]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream('gzip'))
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await new Response(stream).arrayBuffer())
  let bin = ''
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) bin += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000))
  return btoa(bin)
}
export async function gunzipBase64(b64: string): Promise<string> {
  const bin = atob(b64)
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length)
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i)
  const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('gzip'))
  return await new Response(stream).text()
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/types.ts =====
// Data-access interface (AGENT_RULES rule 17). ALL persistence goes through an implementation of this
// interface; nothing else in the app touches storage or the network for data.
//
// The shared implementation (./server.ts) talks to the app's own API, which keeps the data in Postgres:
// a starting snapshot plus an append-only log of changes. When no API answers (the standalone HTML file,
// a static host, local dev without the API), the app runs in memory and says so; nothing is saved.
//
// Only small UI preferences (last tab, the persona you are acting as, the edit passcode) stay in the browser.

export interface RemoteChange { seq: number; actor: string; summary: string; patches: string }
export interface RemoteState { generation: number | null; snapshotGz: string | null; snapshotSeq: number; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }
export type CommitResult = 'ok' | 'conflict' | 'unauthorized' | 'error'

export interface DataAccess {
  readonly kind: 'shared'
  /** null when no API is reachable. */
  health(): Promise<{ editable: boolean } | null>
  /** True when the passcode is accepted for edits. */
  auth(passcode: string): Promise<boolean>
  loadState(schemaVersion: number): Promise<RemoteState>
  changesSince(schemaVersion: number, generation: number, after: number): Promise<{ reset: true } | { reset: false; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }>
  appendChange(passcode: string, c: { generation: number; seq: number; actor: string; summary: string; patches: unknown[] }): Promise<CommitResult>
  addSnapshot(passcode: string, generation: number, seq: number, stateGz: string): Promise<boolean>
  /** Start a new generation from a full snapshot (first edit, or "Reset demo data"). Returns its id. */
  startGeneration(passcode: string, schemaVersion: number, reason: string, actor: string, stateGz: string): Promise<number | null>
  /** Small per-user UI preferences (last tab, filters, passcode). Raw string values, kept in this browser. */
  getPref(key: string): string | null
  setPref(key: string, value: string): void
  removePref(key: string): void
}

===== FILE: src/core/download.ts =====
// Offer a generated file to the viewer as a normal browser download.
export async function saveFile(filename: string, data: string | Blob, mime = 'text/plain'): Promise<'saved' | 'declined' | 'unavailable'> {
  const blob = typeof data === 'string' ? new Blob([data], { type: mime }) : data
  const a = document.createElement('a')
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  a.download = filename
  a.click()
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000)
  return 'saved'
}

/** Rows → CSV text. */
export function toCSV(rows: Record<string, unknown>[]): string {
  if (!rows.length) return ''
  const cols = Object.keys(rows[0])
  const esc = (v: unknown) => { const s = v == null ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s }
  return [cols.join(','), ...rows.map((r) => cols.map((c) => esc(r[c])).join(','))].join('\n')
}

/** A standalone, printable HTML document (for SOPs, gate packets, evidence binders). */
export function htmlDoc(title: string, body: string): string {
  return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${title}</title><style>
  body{font-family:'Public Sans',system-ui,sans-serif;color:#141b24;max-width:900px;margin:40px auto;padding:0 24px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5}
  h1{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 4px}h2{font-size:16px;border-bottom:2px solid #141b24;padding-bottom:4px;margin-top:28px}h3{font-size:14px}
  table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:8px 0}th,td{border:1px solid #d6dce3;padding:5px 8px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}th{background:#e9ecf0;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em}
  td.num{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}.meta{color:#4f5a69}.tag{display:inline-block;border:1px solid #999;border-radius:3px;padding:0 4px;font-size:11px}
  @media print{body{margin:0}}</style></head><body>${body}</body></html>`
}

===== FILE: src/core/sync.ts =====
// Keeps this browser's copy of the data in step with the shared database (through the data-access interface).
//
// - Load: the latest snapshot plus every change after it, replayed with immer.
// - Edit: each local change goes to the server as immer patches, numbered in order. If someone else saved
//   first (409), we undo our pending changes, apply theirs, re-run ours on top and try again.
// - Others' edits: polled every few seconds while the page is visible.
// - No passcode: changes stay on this page only and the app says so. No API: the app runs in memory.
// Only the persona you are acting as (currentUserId) is per browser; everything else is shared.
import { applyPatches, produceWithPatches, type Patch } from 'immer'
import type { AppState } from './types'
import type { DataAccess } from './data/types'
import { buildSeed, SCHEMA_VERSION } from './seed'
import { applyChange, onLocalChange, onReset, replaceState, setSync, strip, useStore, USER_KEY, type LocalChange } from './store'

export const PASSCODE_KEY = 'crosswalk:passcode'
const LOCAL_KEYS = new Set(['currentUserId'])
const SNAPSHOT_EVERY = 50
const POLL_MS = 5000

/** Fields that are secrets (the AI gateway key, map-service tokens): never sent to the shared database. */
const SECRET_KEYS = new Set(['apiKey', 'token'])
/** A deep copy without secret fields. */
export function scrub<T>(v: T): T {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(scrub) as T
  if (v && typeof v === 'object') return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k]) => !SECRET_KEYS.has(k)).map(([k, x]) => [k, scrub(x)])) as T
  return v
}
/** Patches that belong in the shared database: not per-browser fields, not secrets. */
const shared = (ps: Patch[]) => ps
  .filter((p) => !LOCAL_KEYS.has(String(p.path[0])) && !p.path.some((k) => SECRET_KEYS.has(String(k))))
  .map((p) => ('value' in p ? { ...p, value: scrub(p.value) } : p))

export interface SyncDeps {
  data: DataAccess
  gzip: (s: string) => Promise<string>
  gunzip: (s: string) => Promise<string>
  schema?: number
  /** Called when someone else's edits or a reset arrive (for a toast). */
  notify?: (msg: string) => void
}

export function createSync(deps: SyncDeps) {
  const schema = deps.schema ?? SCHEMA_VERSION
  let generation: number | null = null
  let seq = 0
  let passcode = ''
  let canEdit = false
  let queue: LocalChange[] = []
  let flushing = false
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined

  const current = () => strip(useStore.getState())
  const localUser = (s: AppState) => {
    const want = deps.data.getPref(USER_KEY) ?? useStore.getState().currentUserId
    return s.people.some((p) => p.id === want) ? want : s.currentUserId
  }
  const withLocal = (s: AppState): AppState => ({ ...s, currentUserId: localUser(s) })
  const actor = () => useStore.getState().currentUserId
  /** The shared state as of `seq`: the current state with our unsent changes undone. */
  const baseState = () => queue.reduceRight<AppState>((s, c) => applyPatches(s, c.inverse), current())

  async function load(): Promise<void> {
    const r = await deps.data.loadState(schema)
    let s: AppState = r.snapshotGz ? JSON.parse(await deps.gunzip(r.snapshotGz)) : buildSeed()
    for (const c of r.changes) s = applyPatches(s, JSON.parse(c.patches))
    generation = r.generation
    seq = r.head
    queue = []
    replaceState(withLocal(s))
    setSync({ mode: 'shared', unsaved: false, error: null, saving: false })
  }

  async function startGeneration(reason: string, state: AppState): Promise<boolean> {
    const gz = await deps.gzip(JSON.stringify(scrub(state)))
    const id = await deps.data.startGeneration(passcode, schema, reason, actor(), gz)
    if (id == null) return false
    generation = id
    seq = 0
    return true
  }

  async function snapshot() {
    if (generation == null) return
    const g = generation, at = seq
    const gz = await deps.gzip(JSON.stringify(scrub(baseState())))
    await deps.data.addSnapshot(passcode, g, at, gz)
  }

  /** Undo pending changes, apply others' changes, re-run ours on top. */
  async function rebase(): Promise<'ok' | 'reset'> {
    const r = await deps.data.changesSince(schema, generation!, seq)
    if (r.reset) return 'reset'
    let s = baseState()
    for (const c of r.changes) s = applyPatches(s, JSON.parse(c.patches))
    seq = r.head
    const redone: LocalChange[] = []
    for (const c of queue) {
      try {
        const [next, patches, inverse] = produceWithPatches(s, (d: AppState) => applyChange(d, c.fn, c.audit))
        s = next
        if (shared(patches).length) redone.push({ ...c, patches: shared(patches), inverse: shared(inverse) })
      } catch { deps.notify?.('One of your changes no longer applies after someone else’s edit and was dropped.') }
    }
    queue = redone
    replaceState(s)
    return 'ok'
  }

  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
    if (flushing) return
    flushing = true
    setSync({ saving: true })
    try {
      while (queue.length) {
        if (generation == null && !(await startGeneration('First edit', baseState()))) {
          setSync({ error: 'Couldn’t save. Retrying…' }); await wait(4000); continue
        }
        const c = queue[0]
        const res = await deps.data.appendChange(passcode, { generation: generation!, seq: seq + 1, actor: actor(), summary: c.audit?.summary ?? '', patches: c.patches })
        if (res === 'ok') {
          seq += 1
          queue.shift()
          setSync({ error: null, lastSavedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
          if (seq % SNAPSHOT_EVERY === 0) void snapshot()
        } else if (res === 'conflict') {
          if ((await rebase()) === 'reset') { deps.notify?.('The demo data was reset by someone else. Your unsaved change was dropped.'); await load(); break }
        } else if (res === 'unauthorized') {
          lock()
          deps.notify?.('The edit passcode was changed. Enter the new one to keep editing.')
          await load()
          break
        } else {
          setSync({ error: 'Couldn’t save. Retrying…' })
          await wait(4000)
        }
      }
    } finally {
      flushing = false
      setSync({ saving: false })
    }
  }

  function onChange(c: LocalChange) {
    const patches = shared(c.patches)
    if (!patches.length) return
    const mode = useStore.getState().sync.mode
    if (mode !== 'shared') return // offline: memory only, the banner says so
    if (!canEdit) { if (!c.background) setSync({ unsaved: true }); return }
    queue.push({ ...c, patches, inverse: shared(c.inverse) })
    void flush()
  }

  async function poll(): Promise<void> {
